        Starting from the first quarter of 2024, our chief operating decision                                       自二零二四年第一季度起，我們的主要經
        makers (“CODMs”) started to review information under a new reporting                                        營決策者（「主要經營決策者」）開始根據新
        structure, and segment reporting has been updated to conform to                                             的報告結構審閱資料，分部報告亦已根據
        this change. We think the new segment disclosure better reflects our                                        該變化進行更新。我們認為新的分部披露
        business strategies, the development phases of various businesses and                                       更能反映我們的業務策略、各項業務的發
        the financial performance, and better aligns with our resource allocation.                                  展階段及財務表現，也更好地與我們的資
        Our updated reportable segments comprise (i) core OTA, which mainly                                         源分配保持一致。我們已更新的報告分部
        includes the former OTA segment; and (ii) tourism, which mainly                                             包括(i)核心在線旅遊平台，主要包括原在線
        comprising offline travel agency related services and the operation of                                      旅遊平台分部；及(ii)度假，主要包括線下
        scenic park.                                                                                                旅行社相關服務及運營景區。

     OTHER FINANCIAL INFORMATION                                                         其他財務資料
     Non-IFRS Financial Measures                                                         非國際財務報告準則財務計量
     To supplement our consolidated results, which are presented in accordance           為補充根據國際財務報告準則呈列的綜合業
     with IFRS, we also use certain non-IFRS measures, namely adjusted EBITDA            績，我們亦使用若干非國際財務報告準則計量
     and adjusted net profit for the period, as additional financial metrics. These      （即經調整EBITDA及期內經調整溢利淨額）作為
     non-IFRS financial measures are not required by or presented in accordance          額外財務指標。該等非國際財務報告準則財務
     with IFRS.                                                                          計量並非國際財務報告準則所規定，亦並非按
                                                                                         其呈列。


     Adjusted EBITDA is defined as operating profit adjusted for (i) share-based         經調整EBITDA定義為經以下項目調整的經營溢
     compensation, gross; (ii) amortization of intangible assets; (iii) depreciation     利：(i)以股份為基礎的酬金總額；(ii)無形資產攤
     of property, plant and equipment, and right-of-use assets; (iv) foreign             銷；(iii)物業、廠房及設備和使用權資產折舊；
     exchange loss, net; (v) net losses on investees; and (vi) others, including         (iv)匯兌虧損淨額；(v)被投資方的虧損淨額；及
     acquisition-related cost and net gains on foreign currency financial                (vi)其他，包括收購相關成本及外幣金融工具的
     instruments. Adjusted net profit for the period is defined as profit for the        淨收益。期內經調整溢利淨額定義為經以下項
     period adjusted for (i) share-based compensation, net; (ii) the amortization        目調整的期內溢利：(i) 以股份為基礎的酬金淨
     of intangible assets from acquisition; (iii) foreign exchange loss, net; (iv) net   額；(ii)所收購的無形資產攤銷；(iii)匯兌虧損淨
     losses on investees; and (v) others, including acquisition-related cost, net        額；(iv)被投資方的虧損淨額；及(v)其他，包括

     plant and equipment and right-of-use assets from acquisition.                       收購物業、廠房及設備和使用權資產的折舊。


     The above items are excluded from our adjusted EBITDA and adjusted net              上述項目從經調整EBITDA及期內經調整溢利
     profit for the period measures because these items are either non-cash in           淨額的計量中剔除乃由於該等項目屬於非現金
     nature, or are not driven by core operations which render comparisons with          性質，或並非受核心業務所驅動，導致其與過
     prior periods and competitors less meaningful. We believe the adjusted              往期間及競爭對手的比較意義不大。我們認為
     EBITDA and adjusted net profit for the period are useful measures for the           經調整EBITDA及期內經調整溢利淨額對分析師
     analysts and investors as basis for evaluation of our future on-going operating     和投資者而言屬有用的計量指標，可作為評估
     performance as these measures allow more meaningful comparison of our               我們未來持續經營表現的依據，原因是該等計
     performance and projected cash earnings with our historical results from            量指標可讓我們的表現及預測現金收益與我們
     prior periods and to the results of our competitors. Moreover, management           過往期間的歷史業績及競爭對手的業績進行更
     uses these measures internally to evaluate the performance of our business          具意義的比較。此外，管理層內部使用該等計
     as a whole. However, our presentation of such non-IFRS measures may not             量指標來評估我們的整體業務表現。然而，該
     be comparable to similarly titled measures presented by other companies.            等非國際財務報告準則計量的呈列未必可與其
     The use of these non-IFRS measures has limitations as an analytical tool, and       他公司所列類似計量指標相比。該等非國際財

Foreign Exchange Risk                                                            外匯風險
Foreign exchange risk arises when future commercial transactions or              當未來的商業交易或已確認的資產及負債不是
recognized assets and liabilities are denominated in a currency that is not      以我們的功能貨幣計價時，即產生外匯風險。
our functional currency. We manage our foreign exchange risk by performing       我們通過定期審查我們的外匯淨風險敞口管理
regular reviews of our net foreign exchange exposures.                           外匯風險。


Our subsidiaries and Consolidated Affiliated Entities in the PRC operate         我們的中國附屬公司及併表聯屬實體主要在中
mainly in the PRC with most of the transactions settled in RMB. There are        國境內經營，而大部分交易以人民幣結算。
certain financial assets or liabilities of our Group are denominated in the      本集團有若干財務資產或負債以本集團於中國
currencies other than the respective functional currencies of our Group’s        營運的附屬公司及併表聯屬實體各自功能貨幣
subsidiaries and Consolidated Affiliated Entities operating in the PRC, and      以外的貨幣計值，而截至二零二四年六月三十
as of June 30, 2024, the Group entered into certain derivative contracts with    日，本集團與金融機構訂立若干用作經濟對沖
financial institutions which are used for economic hedging purposes. These       用途的衍生合約。該等衍生合約列作衍生金融
derivative contracts are accounted for as derivative financial instruments and   工具並以公允價值計量。
measured at fair value.

Purpose                                                                            目的

and enhance the value of our Company, by linking the personal interests of         本公司任何現時或未來附屬公司或VIE（各定義見
the directors, officers and employees of our Company and any present or            下文）董事、高級職員及僱員的個人利益掛鈎，
future Subsidiaries or VIEs (each term as defined below) of our Company by         藉著激勵該等人士作出傑出表現，為股東帶來
providing such individuals with an incentive for outstanding performance,          豐厚回報，以促進本公司的成功及提升其價
to generate superior returns to the Shareholders. The 2018 Share Incentive         值。二零一八年股份激勵計劃亦旨在使本公司
Plan is further intended to provide flexibility to our Company in its ability to   能靈活激勵、吸引及留用激勵對象提供服務，
motivate, attract, and retain the services of recipients upon whose judgment,      而本公司能否成功經營業務，將主要取決於該

2019 SHARE OPTION PLAN                                                                           二零一九年購股權計劃
The Company has adopted the 2019 Share Option Plan by an ordinary                                本公司已於二零一九年八月二日（「二零一九年
resolution duly passed by the Shareholders on August 2, 2019 (the “Effective                     購股權計劃生效日期」）以股東正式通過的普通
Date of the 2019 Share Option Plan”). During the Reporting Period, no                            決議案採納二零一九年購股權計劃。於報告期
option has been granted under the 2019 Share Option Plan.                                        內，概無根據二零一九年購股權計劃授出購股
                                                                                                 權。


Purpose                                                                                          目的
The purpose of the 2019 Share Option Plan is to provide better rewards to                        二零一九年購股權計劃旨在向本集團過往及未
the talents who have contributed to the development and success of the                           來發展及成功作出貢獻的人才提供更佳回報，
Group, and for the future development of the Group, to motivate such talents                     並通過提供購買本公司股份權益的機會，激勵
to continue to make their best efforts and to attract new talents through                        該等人才繼續全力以赴及招攬新人才。
providing them with the opportunities to acquire interests in Shares of the
Company.

2019 RSU PLAN                                                                     二零一九年受限制股份單位計劃
The Company has approved and adopted the 2019 RSU Plan by the Board’s             本公司已於二零一九年七月二日（「二零一九年
resolution on July 2, 2019 (the “Effective Date of the 2019 RSU Plan”). As at     受 限 制 股 份 單 位 計 劃 生 效 日 期 」）以 董 事 會 決
the beginning and the end of the Reporting Period, no RSUs were granted           議案批准及採納二零一九年受限制股份單位計
but yet to be vested under the 2019 RSU Plan. During the Reporting Period,        劃。於報告期初及期末，概無根據二零一九年
no RSUs were granted, vested, cancelled or lapsed under the 2019 RSU Plan.        受限制股份單位計劃授出但尚未歸屬的受限制
                                                                                  股份單位。於報告期內，概無根據二零一九年
                                                                                  受限制股份單位計劃授出、歸屬、註銷或失效
                                                                                  的受限制股份單位。


Purpose                                                                           目的
The purpose of the 2019 RSU Plan is to provide better rewards to the talents      二零一九年受限制股份單位計劃旨在向本集團
who have contributed to the development and success of the Group, and for         過往及未來發展及成功作出貢獻的人才提供更
the future development of the Group, to motivate such talents to continue         好的回報，通過提供購買本公司股份權益的機
to make their best efforts and to attract new talents through providing them      會，激勵該等人才繼續竭盡所能及招攬新人才。
with the opportunities to acquire interests in Shares of the Company.


Eligible participants                                                             合資格參與者

any other employee of the Group and (iii) any consultant, adviser, agent,         者包括(i)任何董事、(ii)本集團任何其他僱員及
customer, supplier, service provider, business partner or joint venture partner   (iii) 委員會或管理層（視情況而定）全權酌情認
of the Group whom the Committee or the management (as the case may                為已經或將會為本集團作出貢獻的本集團任何
be) considers, in its sole discretion, has contributed or will contribute to      諮詢人、顧問、代理、客戶、供應商、服務提

of the Company) or the management (in respect of persons who are not              員會（就本公司董事及高級管理層而言）或管理
Directors or senior management of the Company) shall have the authority to        層（就非本公司董事或高級管理層之人士而言）
determine which eligible participant will receive RSUs, to grant RSUs and to      有權決定將獲得受限制股份單位的合資格參與
set all terms and conditions of RSUs (including but not limited to vesting and    者、授出受限制股份單位，以及制定受限制股
forfeiture provisions).                                                           份單位的所有條款及條件（包括但不限於歸屬及

2024 SHARE SCHEME                                                                 二零二四年股份計劃
The Company has adopted the 2024 Share Scheme by an ordinary resolution           本公司已於二零二四年六月二十六日以股東正
duly passed by the Shareholders on June 26, 2024. As at the Latest Practicable    式通過的普通決議案採納二零二四年股份計
Date, save for the grant made on June 28, 2024 to Mr. Ma Heping, no share         劃。於最後實際可行日期，除於二零二四年六
options or awards were granted, vested, exercised, lapsed or cancelled under      月二十八日授予馬和平先生外，概無根據二零
the 2024 Share Scheme.                                                            二四年股份計劃授出、歸屬、行使、失效或註
                                                                                  銷購股權或獎勵。


Purpose                                                                           目的
The purpose of the 2024 Share Scheme is (a) to provide the Company                二零二四年股份計劃旨在(a)向本公司提供薪
with flexible means of remunerating, incentivizing, retaining, rewarding,         酬、激勵、挽留、獎勵、補償及 ╱ 或向合資格
compensating and/or providing benefits to Eligible Participants (as defined       參與者（定義見下文）提供利益的靈活方式；(b)
below); (b) to align the interests of Eligible Participants with those of the     通過向合資格參與者提供購買本公司股權的機
Company and Shareholders by providing such Eligible Participants with the         會，使該等合資格參與者的利益與本公司及股
opportunity to acquire shareholding interests in the Company; and (c) to          東的利益保持一致；及(c)鼓勵合資格參與者為

  Financial Information is to be read in conjunction with the annual           與本公司日期為二零二四年三月十九日的

  Accounting Standards and IAS Standards, as well as their amendments          務報表（已按照國際財務報告會計準則及國

3. ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                                        3. 會計政策
  The accounting policies adopted are consistent with those of the             所採用的會計政策與先前財政年度及相應
  previous financial year and corresponding interim reporting period,          中期報告期間所採用的會計政策一致，惟
  except for the adoption of the new or amended standards as set out           下文所載採納新訂或經修訂準則除外。
  below.


  (a) New or amended standards adopted by the Group                            (a) 本集團已採納的新訂或經修訂準
                                                                                   則
      The Group has applied the following new or amended standards for           本集團已於二零二四年一月一日開始
      the first time for the Group’s financial year beginning on January 1,      的財政年度首次應用以下新訂或經修
      2024:                                                                      訂準則：


      •    Classification of Liabilities as Current or Non-current –             •    負債分類為流動或非流動 － 國際

          affect the application of accounting policies and the reported amounts             的呈報金額的判斷、估計及假設。顧名思
          of assets and liabilities, income and expenses. The resulting accounting           義，由此產生的會計估計數很少與相關的
          estimates will, by definition, seldom equal the related actual results.            實際結果相等。

      5. FAIR VALUE ESTIMATION (CONTINUED)                                                      （續）
                                                                                        5. 公允價值估計
          (a) Financial instruments in Level 1                                           (a) 第1層金融工具
              The fair value of financial instruments traded in active markets is          於活躍市場買賣的金融工具的公允價
              based on quoted market prices at the balance sheet date. A market            值乃基於結算日市場報價。倘交易
              is regarded as active if quoted prices are readily and regularly             所、交易商、經紀、行業組織、定價
              available from an exchange, dealer, broker, industry group, pricing          服務機構或監管機構隨時定期提供報
              service, or regulatory agency, and those prices represent actual and         價，且該等價格代表實際定期進行的
              regularly occurring market transactions on an arm’s length basis.            公平市場交易，則市場被視為活躍。


          (b) Financial instruments in Level 2                                           (b) 第2層金融工具
              The fair value of financial instruments that are not traded in an            並無於活躍市場買賣的金融工具公允
              active market is determined by using valuation techniques. These             價值以估值技術釐定。該等估值技術
              valuation techniques maximize the use of observable market                   盡量利用既可觀察市場數據，盡量少
              data where it is available and rely as little as possible on entity          依賴實體的特定估計。倘計算某工具
              specific estimates. If all significant inputs required to fair value an      的公允價值所需的所有重大輸入值為
              instrument are observable, the instrument is included in Level 2.            可觀察數據，則該工具將列入第2層。


          (c) Financial instruments in Level 3                                           (c) 第3層金融工具
              If one or more of the significant inputs is not based on observable          倘一項或多項重大輸入值並非基於可
              market data, the instrument is included in Level 3.                          觀察市場數據，則該工具計入第3層。


              Specific valuation techniques used to value financial instruments            用於評估金融工具的專門估值技術包
              include:                                                                     括：


              •    Quoted market prices or dealer quotes for similar instruments.          •   市場報價或交易商對類似工具的報
                                                                                               價。


              •    Other techniques, such as discounted cash flow analysis, are            •   折現現金流量分析等其他技術用於
                   used to determine fair value for financial instruments.                     釐定金融工具公允價值。


              Level 3 instruments of the Group’s financial assets include                  本集團金融資產的第3層工具包括按公
              long-term or short-term investments measured at fair value through           允價值計量且其變動計入損益的長期
              profit or loss and certain derivative financial instruments.                 或短期投資及若干衍生金融工具。

      6. REVENUE AND SEGMENT INFORMATION                                                   6. 收入及分部資料
          The Group’s business activities, for which discrete financial information         主要營運決策者（「主要營運決策者」）定期
          is available, are regularly reviewed and evaluated by the chief operating         審閱及評估本集團可取得的獨立財務資料
          decision-makers (the “CODMs”). In prior years, the CODMs reviewed the             的業務活動。於過往年度，主要營運決策
          core operating results when making decisions about allocating resources           者在作出有關分配資源及評估本集團整體
          and assessing performance of the Group as a whole (i.e. one reportable            （即一個可呈報分部）表現時會審閱核心經
          segment).                                                                         營業績。


          Starting from January 1, 2024, the Group has set up a new internal                自二零二四年一月一日起，本集團已設立
          reporting structure separating (i) the reporting for the core online travel       一個新的內部報告架構，將(i)核心在線旅遊
          agency and related businesses; and (ii) the reporting for the offline             平台及相關業務的報告；及(ii)線下旅行社
          travel agency and tourism related businesses (which were mainly                   及度假相關業務（主要由本集團於二零二三
          acquired by the Group on November 30, 2023). Accordingly, the CODMs               年十一月三十日收購）的報告分開。因此，
          start to review and evaluate the business performance of these two                主要營運決策者開始分開審閱及評估該兩
          businesses separately for allocating resources and making strategic               項業務的業務表現，以分配資源及作出戰
          decisions. Resulting from this change, the Group has identified two               略決策。由於此項變動，本集團已識別兩
          reportable segments, namely (i) Core OTA segment, which comprising                個可呈報分部，即 (i) 核心在線旅遊平台分
          the core online travel agency and related businesses; and (ii) Tourism            部，包括核心在線旅遊平台及相關業務；
          segment, which comprising the offline travel agency and tourism related           及(ii)度假分部，包括線下旅行社及度假相
          businesses. The segment information, including the comparatives, has              關業務。為應對該變動，分部資料（包括比
          been presented as below in response to this change.                               較數據）已於下文呈列。

      million for the periods ended June 30, 2024 and 2023.                             8.25% 稅率繳納香港所得稅，超過 2 百

          (c) PRC corporate income tax (“CIT”)                                        (c) 中國企業所得稅（「企業所得稅」）
              CIT provision was made on the estimated assessable profits of             截至二零二四年及二零二三年六月三
              entities within the Group incorporated in the PRC for the six months      十日止六個月，於中國註冊成立的本
              ended June 30, 2024 and 2023, calculated in accordance with the           集團內實體的估計應課稅溢利作出企
              relevant regulations of the PRC after considering the available tax       業所得稅撥備，企業所得稅撥備乃經
              benefits from refunds and allowances.                                     考慮來自退稅及補貼的可用稅務優惠
                                                                                        後根據中國相關法規計算。


              Three of the Company’s directly held subsidiaries incorporated in         本公司於中國註冊成立的三間直接持
              the PRC are qualified as High and New Technology Enterprises, and         有附屬公司獲認定為高新技術企業，
              accordingly, they are subject to a reduced preferential CIT rate of       因此根據適用企業所得稅法，該等附

              the applicable CIT law.                                                   六月三十日止六個月已按經削減的企

  Notes:                                                                                    附註：

  (a)   Short-term investments measured at amortized cost                                   (a)   按攤餘成本計量的短期投資

31. RELATED PARTY TRANSACTIONS                                                      31. 關聯方交易
  Parties are considered to be related if one party has the ability, directly            倘一方有能力直接或間接控制另一方，或
  or indirectly, control the other party or exercise significant influence               能對另一方的財務及經營決策行使重大影
  over the other party in making financial and operation decisions. Parties              響力，則雙方被視為關聯方。倘雙方受共
  are also considered to be related if they are subject to common control.               同控制，亦被視為關聯方。本集團主要管
  Members of key management and their close family member of the                         理層成員及彼等的近親亦被視為關聯方。
  Group are also considered as related parties.


  Save as disclosed elsewhere in this Interim Financial Information, the                 除本中期財務資料所披露外，以下重大交
  following significant transactions were carried out between the Group                  易乃由本集團及其關聯方於截至二零二四
  and its related parties during the six months ended June 30, 2024 and                  年及二零二三年六月三十日止六個月期間
  2023. In the opinion of the Directors of the Company, the related party                進行。本公司董事認為，該等關聯方交易
  transactions were carried out in the normal course of business and at                  乃於日常業務過程中按本集團與各自關聯
  terms negotiated between the Group and the respective related parties.                 方磋商的條款進行。
